1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	
4	cd /user_4813494d/openbmb
5	
6	# Ensure the project venv is active (scripts must not depend on caller's shell).
7	SALA_VENV="/opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env"
8	if [[ "${VIRTUAL_ENV:-}" != "${SALA_VENV}" || "$(command -v python3)" != "${SALA_VENV}/bin/python3" ]]; then
9	    # shellcheck disable=SC1091
10	    source "${SALA_VENV}/bin/activate"
11	fi
12	
13	# EAGLE-3 speculative decoding server (current production config)
14	# - spec_steps=3, topk=2, dtn=7  (chain verify, default D5 mode)
15	# - dynamic spec mode: NO_SPEC bs>=32, D7 bs<=1, D5 otherwise (theta 0.85/0.5)
16	# - ngram route: hit -> chain verify branch, miss -> EAGLE draft, both under cuda graph
17	# - target: NVFP4 (GPTQ + FourOverSix), hybrid Marlin/CUTLASS decode
18	# - draft:  det_prefill NVFP4 QAT, b12x explicitly off by default
19	#
20	# 只显式设置与 code 默认不同的 env；其余使用 code 默认值（参见
21	# sglang/srt/{environ.py,speculative/spec_mode.py,layers/.../*}）。
22	# 用户可在调用前 export 任一 SGLANG_*/EAGLE_* env 来覆盖。
23	
24	SPEC_STEPS="${EAGLE_SPEC_STEPS:-3}"
25	TOPK="${EAGLE_TOPK:-2}"
26	# dtn = 1 + topk * spec_steps (tree nodes)
27	DTN=$((1 + TOPK * SPEC_STEPS))
28	TARGET_MODEL="${EAGLE_TARGET_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det}"
29	DRAFT_MODEL="${EAGLE_DRAFT_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/models/v3mix_300k_s6250_ind672}"
30	# MiniCPM-SALA sliding-window draft prefill: 仅最末 N tokens 进 draft prefill。
31	# 0 = 关闭(全量 draft prefill)。长上下文吞吐受益大(524K -> 32K 后 draft prefill ~16× 加速)。
32	# EAGLE-3 chain verify 无损,target 主链不受影响。
33	DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N="${EAGLE_DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N:-32768}"
34	# MiniCPM-SALA draft sliding-window attention: draft 自己的 1 层 attention 只读
35	# 最末 N tokens KV(真截断 kv_indices,不是 mask)。配合 prefix_last_n 用,且必须
36	# >= LAST_N + chunk_size(8192) 留 margin,保证 window 起点严格落在末 chunk 已写
37	# 入的 KV 范围(末 chunk 极端 1-token 时也安全)。0 = full attention。
38	DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW="${EAGLE_DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW:-40960}"
39	MARS_THETA="${EAGLE_MARS_THETA:-1}"
40	D5_MARS_THETA="${EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA:-0.85}"
41	D7_MARS_THETA="${EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA:-0.5}"
42	RANDOM_SEED_ARGS=()
43	if [[ -n "${SGLANG_RANDOM_SEED:-}" ]]; then
44	    RANDOM_SEED_ARGS=(--random-seed "${SGLANG_RANDOM_SEED}")
45	fi
46	
47	echo "[start_eagle] MARS global=${MARS_THETA} D5=${D5_MARS_THETA} D7=${D7_MARS_THETA}"
48	
49	SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE="${SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json}" \
50	SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD="${SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD:-48}" \
51	CUTE_DSL_ARCH="${CUTE_DSL_ARCH:-sm_120a}" \
52	CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR="${CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/b12x_aot_cache}" \
53	SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X="${SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X:-0}" \
54	SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP="${SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP:-1}" \
55	SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL="${SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL:-1}" \
56	EAGLE_MARS_THETA="${MARS_THETA}" \
57	EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA="${D5_MARS_THETA}" \
58	EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA="${D7_MARS_THETA}" \
59	EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE="${EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE:-1}" \
60	EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS="${EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS:-32}" \
61	EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS="${EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS:-28}" \
62	EAGLE_D7_BS="${EAGLE_D7_BS:-1}" \
63	EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS="${EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS:-3}" \
64	EAGLE_D5_TOPK="${EAGLE_D5_TOPK:-2}" \
65	EAGLE_D5_STEPS="${EAGLE_D5_STEPS:-3}" \
66	EAGLE_D5_DTN="${EAGLE_D5_DTN:-7}" \
67	EAGLE_D7_TOPK="${EAGLE_D7_TOPK:-2}" \
68	EAGLE_D7_STEPS="${EAGLE_D7_STEPS:-5}" \
69	EAGLE_D7_DTN="${EAGLE_D7_DTN:-11}" \
70	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE="${SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE:-1}" \
71	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_LOG_EVERY="${SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_LOG_EVERY:-0}" \
72	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MIN_MATCH="${SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MIN_MATCH:-5}" \
73	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_MATCH="${SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_MATCH:-12}" \
74	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS="${SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS:-15}" \
75	python3 -m sglang.launch_server \
76	    --model-path "${TARGET_MODEL}" \
77	    --trust-remote-code --port 30000 \
78	    --mem-fraction-static 0.75 \
79	    --max-running-requests 36 \
80	    --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer \
81	    --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --disable-radix-cache \
82	    --skip-server-warmup \
83	    --quantization modelopt_fp4 \
84	    --speculative-algorithm EAGLE3 \
85	    --speculative-num-steps "${SPEC_STEPS}" \
86	    --speculative-eagle-topk "${TOPK}" \
87	    --speculative-num-draft-tokens "${DTN}" \
88	    --speculative-draft-attention-backend flashinfer \
89	    --speculative-draft-model-path "${DRAFT_MODEL}" \
90	    --speculative-draft-prefix-last-n "${DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N}" \

1	# Ngram Routing Runtime 集成（2026-05-15）
2	
3	## 结论
4	
5	本次把 per-request in-context ngram lookup 接入 EAGLE-3 runtime：
6	
7	- ngram 命中：直接构造一条 chain verify 分支，跳过 draft model forward。
8	- ngram 未命中：保持原 EAGLE-3 draft path。
9	- 命中和未命中可以混在同一个 decode batch，最终合并成一个 `EagleVerifyInput` 交给 target verify。
10	- 提交包默认开启 routing，关闭周期统计日志；CUDA graph 仍保持开启。
11	
12	提交包指 `demo-sala/` 比赛提交路径。`eval/start_eagle.sh` 只作为本地对齐验证入口，最终平台消费的是 `demo-sala/prepare_env.sh` 导出的 server 参数和环境变量。
13	
14	## 默认配置
15	
16	`eval/start_eagle.sh` 与 `demo-sala/prepare_env.sh` 对齐：
17	
18	| 项 | 默认 |
19	|---|---|
20	| target | `/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det`（本地）/ 提交包传入模型 |
21	| draft | `demo-sala/data/eagle_draft` |
22	| EAGLE base | `spec_steps=3, topk=2, dtn=7` |
23	| dynamic spec | `NO_SPEC_BS=32`, `D5=(steps=3, topk=2, dtn=7)`, `D7=(steps=5, topk=2, dtn=11)` |
24	| MARS | global `1`, D5 `0.85`, D7 `0.5` |
25	| ngram route | `SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE=1` |
26	| ngram config | `k=5..12`, `K=15` |
27	| ngram log | `SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_LOG_EVERY=0` |
28	
29	可覆盖环境变量：
30	
31	```bash
32	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE=0        # 关闭 routing
33	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_LOG_EVERY=1000 # 打开周期命中率日志
34	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MIN_MATCH=5
35	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_MATCH=12
36	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS=15
37	```
38	
39	## 实现路径
40	
41	主要文件：
42	
43	- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_worker.py`
44	- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_draft_cuda_graph_runner.py`
45	- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_draft_extend_cuda_graph_runner.py`
46	- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py`
47	- `demo-sala/prepare_env.sh`
48	- `eval/start_eagle.sh`
49	
50	关键设计：
51	
52	1. 每个 req 在 `origin_input_ids + output_ids` 上做最长后缀 lookup。
53	2. lookup 命中时，构造线性 chain：`verified_id + draft_tokens[:K]`。
54	3. lookup 未命中时，按 miss indices 建 sub-batch，走原 EAGLE draft。
55	4. hit/miss rows 合并到一个 `EagleVerifyInput`，target verify 一次完成。
56	5. `forward_draft_extend_after_decode` 按实际 accept length 扩展 token budget，支持 ngram 接受长度大于 EAGLE nominal steps。
57	
58	未采用现有 `NGRAMWorker`，因为它维护全局 `NgramCache`，会跨 response 存 suffix；本路线只做 request-local lookup。
59	
60	## CUDA Graph 稳定性修复
61	
62	复现过的崩溃：
63	
64	```text
65	torch.AcceleratorError: CUDA error: an illegal memory access was encountered
66	```
67	
68	表面栈落在 `_build_ngram_chain_verify_input()` 的 `torch.tensor(...)`，但开启同步定位后，真实触发点在 draft CUDA graph replay。
69	
70	根因判断：
71	
72	- dynamic spec 的 D5 和 D7 规格不同。
73	- 旧实现共享一个 draft decode attention backend。
74	- D5/D7 都可能 capture `bs=1`，FlashInfer backend 内部 per-bs CUDA graph metadata/wrapper 被后一次 capture 覆盖。
75	- D5 graph replay 可能拿到 D7 backend metadata，造成 multistep KV index layout 和 graph capture 不一致。
76	
77	修复：
78	
79	- D5 / D7 分别创建 draft decode attention backend。
80	- D5 / D7 分别 capture CUDA graph，runner 绑定 capture 时的 backend。
81	- `_apply_spec_config()` 切 mode 时同时切 runner 和 backend。
82	- ngram routing 下 miss sub-batch 可以是任意 batch size，因此 draft graph capture 全整数 `bs=1..max_bs`，避免 dummy padding row 进入 FlashInfer KV index 构造。
83	- draft extend graph 的 per-bs token budget 扩到 ngram 最大接受深度。
84	- Mamba verify state copy kernel 增加边界检查。
85	
86	## 验证记录
87	
88	编译验证：
89	
90	```bash
91	python3 -m py_compile \
92	  demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_worker.py \
93	  demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_draft_cuda_graph_runner.py \
94	  demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_draft_extend_cuda_graph_runner.py \
95	  demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py
96	```
97	
98	稳定性验证：
99	
100	```bash
101	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE=1 \
102	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_DEBUG_SYNC=1 \
103	SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_LOG_EVERY=1000 \
104	bash eval/start_eagle.sh
105	
106	python3 bench/collect_real_outputs.py \
107	  --api-base http://127.0.0.1:30000 \
108	  --input bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl \
109	  --output outputs/ngram_real_outputs/bench64_separate_backend_debugsync_20260515_175258.jsonl \
110	  --concurrency 16 \
111	  --timeout 3600
112	```
113	
114	结果：`success=64/64`，长输出样本包括 8K、10K、14K、30K token 级别，未再复现 illegal memory access；server log 中 decode path 仍为 `cuda graph: True`。
115	
116	生产默认不打开 `SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_DEBUG_SYNC`，也不打开周期 route 日志。
117
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	# =============================================================
4	# mini_bench.sh — 快速速度验证
5	#
6	# 用法:
7	#   bash bench/mini_bench.sh [API_BASE] [N_S1] [N_S8] [N_SMAX]
8	#
9	# 默认值:
10	#   API_BASE = http://127.0.0.1:30000
11	#   N_S1=8  N_S8=24  N_SMAX=64
12	#
13	# S8=24 覆盖三个满批，能体现并发压力；Smax=64 覆盖长尾。
14	# 数据集嵌套关系: S1(8) ⊆ S8(24) ⊆ Smax(64)，每档样本唯一。
15	# 本脚本取各档前 N 条，保留与全量相同的长尾特性。
16	# =============================================================
17	
18	API_BASE="${1:-http://127.0.0.1:30000}"
19	N_S1="${2:-12}"
20	N_S8="${3:-36}"
21	N_SMAX="${4:-96}"
22	
23	BENCH_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
24	DATA_DIR="${BENCH_DIR}/data_full"
25	TOOLKIT_BENCH="${BENCH_DIR}/../toolkit/bench_serving.sh"
26	
27	echo "=========================================================="
28	echo "  mini_bench  S1=${N_S1}  S8=${N_S8}  Smax=${N_SMAX}"
29	echo "  API: ${API_BASE}"
30	echo "=========================================================="
31	
32	# 分层抽样到 /tmp（按 prompt_tokens 分布均匀取样，避免前段全是短请求）
33	S1_MINI="/tmp/mini_s1.jsonl"
34	S8_MINI="/tmp/mini_s8.jsonl"
35	SMAX_MINI="/tmp/mini_smax.jsonl"
36	
37	python3 - <<PY
38	import json, random
39	
40	SEED = 42
41	
42	def stratified_sample(src, dst, n):
43	    if n <= 0:
44	        open(dst, 'w').close()  # empty file
45	        return
46	    rng = random.Random(SEED)
47	    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(src)]
48	    items.sort(key=lambda x: x['prompt_tokens'])
49	    step = len(items) / n
50	    sampled = [rng.choice(items[int(i*step):int((i+1)*step) or len(items)]) for i in range(n)]
51	    with open(dst, 'w') as f:
52	        for it in sampled:
53	            f.write(json.dumps(it, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')
54	    pts = [it['prompt_tokens'] for it in sampled]
55	    print(f"  {dst}: n={n}, prompt avg={sum(pts)//n}, min={min(pts)}, max={max(pts)}")
56	
57	stratified_sample("${DATA_DIR}/speed_bench_c1.jsonl",         "${S1_MINI}",   ${N_S1})
58	stratified_sample("${DATA_DIR}/speed_bench_c8.jsonl",         "${S8_MINI}",   ${N_S8})
59	stratified_sample("${DATA_DIR}/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl", "${SMAX_MINI}", ${N_SMAX})
60	PY
61	
62	echo "  抽样完成: S1=${N_S1}条  S8=${N_S8}条  Smax=${N_SMAX}条"
63	echo ""
64	
65	SPEED_DATA_S1="${S1_MINI}" \
66	SPEED_DATA_S8="${S8_MINI}" \
67	SPEED_DATA_SMAX="${SMAX_MINI}" \
68	bash "${TOOLKIT_BENCH}" "${API_BASE}"
69

1	#!/bin/bash
2	# ============================================================
3	# benchmark_duration 评测脚本（基于 sglang.bench_serving）
4	#
5	# 使用 sglang 官方 bench_serving 工具，在 3 档并发度下
6	# 分别跑完所有评测请求，记录 Benchmark Duration。
7	#
8	# 用法（容器内由 entrypoint.sh 调用）:
9	#   bash /app/bench_serving.sh <API_BASE>
10	#
11	# 环境变量（可选）:
12	#   SPEED_DATA_S1   - S1(并发度1) 数据集路径（不设则跳过该项测试）
13	#   SPEED_DATA_S8   - S8(并发度8) 数据集路径（不设则跳过该项测试）
14	#   SPEED_DATA_SMAX - Smax(不设并发上限) 数据集路径（不设则跳过该项测试）
15	#
16	# 参数:
17	#   API_BASE     - SGLang API 地址，如 http://127.0.0.1:30000
18	# ============================================================
19	set -e
20	
21	API_BASE="${1:?用法: bash bench_serving.sh <API_BASE>}"
22	
23	# 解析 host 和 port
24	HOST=$(echo "${API_BASE}" | sed -E 's|https?://||' | cut -d: -f1)
25	PORT=$(echo "${API_BASE}" | sed -E 's|https?://||' | cut -d: -f2)
26	
27	echo "[bench_serving] API: ${API_BASE} (host=${HOST}, port=${PORT})"
28	DATA_S1="${SPEED_DATA_S1:-}"
29	DATA_S8="${SPEED_DATA_S8:-}"
30	DATA_SMAX="${SPEED_DATA_SMAX:-}"
31	
32	echo "[bench_serving] 数据集:"
33	[ -n "${DATA_S1}" ] && echo "  S1: ${DATA_S1}" || echo "  S1: (未指定，跳过)"
34	[ -n "${DATA_S8}" ] && echo "  S8: ${DATA_S8}" || echo "  S8: (未指定，跳过)"
35	[ -n "${DATA_SMAX}" ] && echo "  Smax: ${DATA_SMAX}" || echo "  Smax: (未指定，跳过)"
36	
37	if [ -z "${DATA_S1}" ] && [ -z "${DATA_S8}" ] && [ -z "${DATA_SMAX}" ]; then
38	    echo "[bench_serving] 未指定任何速度评测数据集，跳过 benchmark_duration"
39	    echo '{"S1":0,"S8":0,"Smax":0}'
40	    exit 0
41	fi
42	
43	# ============================================================
44	# Step 1: 转换数据集格式（逐档位）
45	# speed_*.jsonl -> custom 格式（conversations）
46	# ============================================================
47	convert_dataset() {
48	    local input_file="$1"
49	    local output_file="$2"
50	
51	    python3 - "$input_file" "$output_file" <<'PY'
52	import json
53	import sys
54	
55	input_file = sys.argv[1]
56	output_file = sys.argv[2]
57	
58	def get_prompt(item):
59	    # 兼容不同字段命名（历史数据/新数据）
60	    return item.get("question") or item.get("input") or item.get("prompt")
61	
62	n = 0
63	with open(input_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fin, open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fout:
64	    for line_num, line in enumerate(fin, 1):
65	        line = line.strip()
66	        if not line:
67	            continue
68	        item = json.loads(line)
69	        prompt = get_prompt(item)
70	        if prompt is None:
71	            raise KeyError(f"Missing prompt field at line {line_num}. Need one of: input/question/prompt")
72	
73	        converted = {
74	            "conversations": [
75	                {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
76	                {"role": "assistant", "content": item.get("model_response", "placeholder")},
77	            ]
78	        }
79	        fout.write(json.dumps(converted, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
80	        n += 1
81	
82	print(n)
83	PY
84	}
85	
86	# ============================================================
87	# Step 2: 在 3 档并发度下分别运行 bench_serving
88	# ============================================================
89	RESULT_JSON='{"S1":0,"S8":0,"Smax":0}'
90	
91	BENCH_CONFIGS=""
92	[ -n "${DATA_S1}" ] && BENCH_CONFIGS="${BENCH_CONFIGS} S1:1"
93	[ -n "${DATA_S8}" ] && BENCH_CONFIGS="${BENCH_CONFIGS} S8:8"
94	[ -n "${DATA_SMAX}" ] && BENCH_CONFIGS="${BENCH_CONFIGS} Smax:"
95	
96	for CONFIG in ${BENCH_CONFIGS}; do
97	    LABEL=$(echo "${CONFIG}" | cut -d: -f1)
98	    CONC=$(echo "${CONFIG}" | cut -d: -f2)
99	
100	    # 选择该档位的数据集
101	    if [ "${LABEL}" = "S1" ]; then
102	        DATASET_PATH="${DATA_S1}"
103	    elif [ "${LABEL}" = "S8" ]; then
104	        DATASET_PATH="${DATA_S8}"
105	    elif [ "${LABEL}" = "Smax" ]; then
106	        DATASET_PATH="${DATA_SMAX}"
107	    fi
108	
109	    if [ ! -f "${DATASET_PATH}" ]; then
110	        echo "  [${LABEL}] 数据集文件不存在: ${DATASET_PATH}，跳过"
111	        continue
112	    fi
113	
114	    # 转换数据集（每档位独立转换，避免相互影响）
115	    CONVERTED_DATA="/tmp/bench_eval_data_${LABEL}.jsonl"
116	    echo "[bench_serving] [${LABEL}] 数据集: ${DATASET_PATH}"
117	    convert_dataset "${DATASET_PATH}" "${CONVERTED_DATA}" >/dev/null
118	    NUM_PROMPTS=$(wc -l < "${CONVERTED_DATA}" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
119	    echo "[bench_serving] [${LABEL}] 转换完成: ${NUM_PROMPTS} 条 -> ${CONVERTED_DATA}"
120	
121	    echo ""
122	    echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
123	    if [ -n "${CONC}" ]; then
124	        echo "  [${LABEL}] 开始测试 - 并发度: ${CONC}, 共 ${NUM_PROMPTS} 条请求"
125	    else
126	        echo "  [${LABEL}] 开始测试 - 无并发上限, 共 ${NUM_PROMPTS} 条请求"
127	    fi
128	    echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
129	
130	    if [ "${NUM_PROMPTS}" = "0" ]; then
131	        echo "  [${LABEL}] 无有效请求（NUM_PROMPTS=0），跳过该档位"
132	        DURATION="0"
133	        RESULT_JSON=$(python3 -c "
134	import json
135	result = json.loads('${RESULT_JSON}')
136	result['${LABEL}'] = float('${DURATION}')
137	print(json.dumps(result))
138	")
139	        continue
140	    fi
141	
142	    # 构建 bench_serving 命令
143	    BENCH_CMD="python3 -m sglang.bench_serving \
144	        --backend sglang \
145	        --host ${HOST} \
146	        --port ${PORT} \
147	        --dataset-name custom \
148	        --dataset-path ${CONVERTED_DATA} \
149	        --num-prompts ${NUM_PROMPTS} \
150	        --flush-cache"
151	
152	    # 有并发限制时加 --max-concurrency，否则不加（全部请求同时发送）
153	    if [ -n "${CONC}" ]; then
154	        BENCH_CMD="${BENCH_CMD} --max-concurrency ${CONC}"
155	    fi
156	
157	    BENCH_OUTPUT=$(eval ${BENCH_CMD} 2>&1) || true
158	
159	    echo "${BENCH_OUTPUT}"
160	
161	    # 提取 Benchmark duration (s): 行的值
162	    DURATION=$(echo "${BENCH_OUTPUT}" | grep -oP 'Benchmark duration \(s\):\s+\K[0-9.]+' || echo "0")
163	
164	    echo "  [${LABEL}] Benchmark duration: ${DURATION}s"
165	
166	    # 追加到结果 JSON
167	    RESULT_JSON=$(python3 -c "
168	import json
169	result = json.loads('${RESULT_JSON}')
170	result['${LABEL}'] = float('${DURATION}')
171	print(json.dumps(result))
172	")
173	done
174	
175	# ============================================================
176	# Step 3: 输出汇总
177	# ============================================================
178	echo ""
179	echo "============================================================"
180	echo "  Benchmark Duration 汇总"
181	echo "============================================================"
182	
183	python3 -c "
184	import json
185	result = json.loads('${RESULT_JSON}')
186	for key in ['S1', 'S8', 'Smax']:
187	    print(f'  {key:>4s}: {result.get(key, 0):>10.2f}s')
188	"
189	
190	echo "============================================================"
191	
192	# 最后一行输出 JSON（供 entrypoint.sh 捕获）
193	echo "${RESULT_JSON}"
194

我把生产配置和当前可调旋钮梳理清楚了，先汇报理解，再确认参数搜索范围。

## 当前生产配置（`eval/start_eagle.sh`）

**Server 启动参数**
- target: `MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det`（NVFP4，hybrid Marlin decode，threshold=48）
- draft: `v3mix_300k_s6250_ind672`（注：start_eagle.sh 默认指向 v3mix 300K 训出的新 baseline，与 docs 里说的 `v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757` 已经不一致 —— 提交包 `demo-sala/data/eagle_draft/` 才是 v2mix；start_eagle.sh 是本地新 draft 验证入口）
- 推测解码主线：EAGLE-3 chain，`spec_steps=3, topk=2, dtn=7`
- `--chunked-prefill-size 8192`，`--mem-fraction-static 0.75`，`--max-running-requests 36`

**Dynamic spec mode（按 running batch size 切档）**
- `bs ≥ 32` → NO_SPEC（关 spec，纯 target decode）；`leave_bs=28` 滞回
- `1 < bs < 32` → D5：steps=3, topk=2, dtn=7
- `bs ≤ 1` → D7：steps=5, topk=2, dtn=11；`leave_bs=3` 滞回

**MARS verify（接受阈值）**
- global θ=1，D5 θ=0.85，D7 θ=0.5 → **这是你说不能动的精度旋钮**

**Draft sliding window**
- `DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N=32768`（draft prefill 只取末 32K token，长上下文吞吐 ~16× 加速）
- `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW=40960`（draft 1 层 attention 真截断 KV，必须 ≥ prefix_last_n + 8192）
- EAGLE chain verify 无损，这些不影响精度

**Ngram routing**（提交包默认开）
- `MIN_MATCH=5`, `MAX_MATCH=12`, `MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS=15`
- 命中走 chain verify 分支，未命中走 EAGLE draft —— target 永远 verify，**无损**

## 可搜索的旋钮（不动精度）

按你的红线（MARS θ + batch 切换阈值不动）：

| 类别 | 参数 | 当前值 | 候选范围 | 精度影响 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ngram | `MIN_MATCH` | 5 | 3–8 | 无（target verify） |
| ngram | `MAX_MATCH` | 12 | 8–20 | 无 |
| ngram | `MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS` | 15 | 8–24 | 无 |
| draft window | `DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N` | 32768 | 8K/16K/32K/64K | 无（chain verify） |
| draft window | `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW` | 40960 | prefix_last_n + 8192 联动 | 无 |
| spec 形状 | D5/D7 `steps/topk/dtn` | 3·2/5·2 | 2·2、3·3、4·2 等 | 无（chain verify 无损，只是接受率 vs verify cost 的 trade-off） |
| decode kernel | `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD` | 48 | 16/32/48/64/96 | 无 |
| 备用 | `SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X` | 0 | 0/1 | 无（draft graph 兼容已修，曾因兼容关掉） |

1328	    def _mask_row_offset(self, seq_lens_cpu: torch.Tensor, row: int, dtn: int) -> int:
1329	        if row == 0:
1330	            prefix = 0
1331	        else:
1332	            prefix = int(seq_lens_cpu[:row].sum().item())
1333	        return prefix * dtn + row * dtn * dtn
1334	
1335	    def _fill_ngram_chain_mask_row(
1336	        self,
1337	        tree_mask: torch.Tensor,
1338	        offset: int,
1339	        seq_len: int,
1340	        dtn: int,
1341	        chain_len: int,
1342	    ) -> None:
1343	        row_width = seq_len + dtn
1344	        tree_mask[offset : offset + dtn * row_width].fill_(True)
1345	        for q in range(dtn):
1346	            tree_start = offset + q * row_width + seq_len
1347	            tree_mask[tree_start : tree_start + dtn].fill_(False)
1348	            visible_to = min(q, chain_len)
1349	            tree_mask[tree_start : tree_start + visible_to + 1].fill_(True)
1350	
1351	    def _build_ngram_chain_verify_input(
1352	        self,
1353	        batch: ScheduleBatch,
1354	        ngram_drafts: List[Optional[List[int]]],
1355	        miss_indices: List[int],
1356	        miss_spec_info: Optional[EagleVerifyInput],
1357	    ) -> EagleVerifyInput:
1358	        bs = len(batch.reqs)
1359	        dtn = self.speculative_num_draft_tokens
1360	        device = batch.seq_lens.device
1361	        seq_lens_cpu = batch.seq_lens_cpu
1362	        seq_lens_sum = int(seq_lens_cpu.sum().item())
1363	
1364	        draft_tokens = torch.empty((bs * dtn,), dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1365	        positions = torch.empty((bs * dtn,), dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1366	        retrive_index = torch.empty((bs, dtn), dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1367	        retrive_next_token = torch.full((bs, dtn), -1, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1368	        retrive_next_sibling = torch.full((bs, dtn), -1, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1369	        tree_mask = torch.empty(
1370	            (seq_lens_sum * dtn + dtn * dtn * bs,),
1371	            dtype=torch.bool,
1372	            device=device,
1373	        )
1374	
1375	        draft_view = draft_tokens.view(bs, dtn)
1376	        pos_view = positions.view(bs, dtn)
1377	        verified_id_cpu = [
1378	            int(req.output_ids[-1] if req.output_ids else req.origin_input_ids[-1])
1379	            for req in batch.reqs
1380	        ]
1381	
1382	        miss_row_by_index = {idx: row for row, idx in enumerate(miss_indices)}
1383	        miss_draft_view = (
1384	            miss_spec_info.draft_token.view(len(miss_indices), dtn)
1385	            if miss_spec_info is not None
1386	            else None
1387	        )
1388	        miss_pos_view = (
1389	            miss_spec_info.positions.view(len(miss_indices), dtn)
1390	            if miss_spec_info is not None
1391	            else None
1392	        )
1393	
1394	        for i in range(bs):
1395	            full_offset = self._mask_row_offset(seq_lens_cpu, i, dtn)
1396	            row_len = int(seq_lens_cpu[i].item()) + dtn
1397	            if i in miss_row_by_index:
1398	                miss_row = miss_row_by_index[i]
1399	                draft_view[i].copy_(miss_draft_view[miss_row])
1400	                pos_view[i].copy_(miss_pos_view[miss_row])
1401	                delta = (i - miss_row) * dtn
1402	                retrive_index[i].copy_(miss_spec_info.retrive_index[miss_row] + delta)
1403	                retrive_next_token[i].copy_(miss_spec_info.retrive_next_token[miss_row])
1404	                retrive_next_sibling[i].copy_(
1405	                    miss_spec_info.retrive_next_sibling[miss_row]
1406	                )
1407	
1408	                miss_offset = self._mask_row_offset(
1409	                    miss_spec_info.seq_lens_cpu, miss_row, dtn
1410	                )
1411	                tree_mask[full_offset : full_offset + dtn * row_len].copy_(
1412	                    miss_spec_info.custom_mask[
1413	                        miss_offset : miss_offset + dtn * row_len
1414	                    ]
1415	                )
1416	                continue
1417	
1418	            draft = ngram_drafts[i]
1419	            assert draft is not None
1420	            chain_len = min(len(draft), dtn - 1)
1421	            seq_len = int(seq_lens_cpu[i].item())
1422	            row_tokens = [int(verified_id_cpu[i])] + [int(x) for x in draft[:chain_len]]
1423	            if len(row_tokens) < dtn:
1424	                row_tokens.extend([row_tokens[-1]] * (dtn - len(row_tokens)))
1425	            draft_view[i].copy_(
1426	                torch.tensor(row_tokens, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1427	            )
1428	            pos_view[i].copy_(
1429	                torch.tensor(
1430	                    [seq_len + min(j, chain_len) for j in range(dtn)],
1431	                    dtype=torch.long,
1432	                    device=device,
1433	                )
1434	            )
1435	            retrive_index[i].copy_(
1436	                torch.arange(i * dtn, (i + 1) * dtn, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
1437	            )
1438	            if chain_len > 0:
1439	                retrive_next_token[i, :chain_len] = torch.arange(
1440	                    1, chain_len + 1, dtype=torch.long, device=device
1441	                )
1442	            self._fill_ngram_chain_mask_row(
1443	                tree_mask, full_offset, seq_len, dtn, chain_len
1444	            )
1445	
1446	        return EagleVerifyInput(
1447	            draft_token=draft_tokens,
1448	            custom_mask=tree_mask,
1449	            positions=positions,
1450	            retrive_index=retrive_index,
1451	            [REDACTED],
1452	            retrive_next_sibling=retrive_next_sibling,
1453	            retrive_cum_len=None,
1454	            spec_steps=max(self.speculative_num_steps, dtn - 1),
1455	            topk=self.topk,
1456	            draft_token_num=dtn,
1457	            capture_hidden_mode=CaptureHiddenMode.FULL,
1458	            seq_lens_sum=seq_lens_sum,
1459	            seq_lens_cpu=seq_lens_cpu,
1460	            mars_theta=self._active_mars_theta,
1461	        )

1463	    def _draft_ngram_route(self, batch: ScheduleBatch) -> Optional[EagleVerifyInput]:
1464	        dtn = self.speculative_num_draft_tokens
1465	        max_draft_tokens = min(self.ngram_route_max_draft_tokens, dtn - 1)
1466	        if max_draft_tokens <= 0:
1467	            return None
1468	
1469	        ngram_drafts = [
1470	            self._lookup_req_ngram_draft(req, max_draft_tokens)
1471	            for req in batch.reqs
1472	        ]
1473	        hit_indices = [i for i, draft in enumerate(ngram_drafts) if draft]
1474	        if not hit_indices:
1475	            return None
1476	
1477	        bs = len(batch.reqs)
1478	        hit_index_set = set(hit_indices)
1479	        miss_indices = [i for i in range(bs) if i not in hit_index_set]
1480	        miss_spec_info = None
1481	        miss_batch = None
1482	        if miss_indices:
1483	            miss_batch = self._make_ngram_route_subbatch(batch, miss_indices)
1484	            miss_spec_info = self._draft_eagle_verify_input(miss_batch)
1485	            _ngram_debug_cuda_sync("route_miss_eagle_draft")
1486	            batch.out_cache_loc = miss_batch.out_cache_loc
1487	        else:
1488	            batch.out_cache_loc = None
1489	
1490	        self._ngram_route_step_ct += bs
1491	        self._ngram_route_hit_ct += len(hit_indices)
1492	        if len(hit_indices) == bs:
1493	            self._ngram_route_all_hit_ct += 1
1494	        elif hit_indices:
1495	            self._ngram_route_mixed_ct += 1
1496	        if (
1497	            self._ngram_route_log_every > 0
1498	            and self._ngram_route_step_ct >= self._ngram_route_log_every
1499	        ):
1500	            logger.info(
1501	                "[eagle-ngram-route] req_steps=%d hit_req_steps=%d "
1502	                "hit_rate=%.2f%% mixed_batches=%d all_hit_batches=%d",
1503	                self._ngram_route_step_ct,
1504	                self._ngram_route_hit_ct,
1505	                100.0 * self._ngram_route_hit_ct / max(1, self._ngram_route_step_ct),
1506	                self._ngram_route_mixed_ct,

1508	            )
1509	            self._ngram_route_step_ct = 0

1	"""Dynamic speculative-decoding mode selection for MiniCPM-SALA.
2	
3	Picks one of three modes per decode step based on the running batch size:
4	
5	    NO_SPEC      : bs >= EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS         (target_worker direct decode)
6	    MARS_D5      : default spec config            (chain verify, dtn=5 by default)
7	    MARS_D7      : bs <= EAGLE_D7_BS              (chain verify, dtn=7 by default)
8	
9	Hysteresis (separate enter/leave thresholds) prevents prefill bursts from
10	flipping NO_SPEC mid-bench. Bench traffic is monotonically decreasing on bs,
11	so transitions are essentially one-way: NO_SPEC -> MARS_D5 -> MARS_D7.
12	
13	All thresholds and per-mode spec parameters are env-driven so we can sweep
14	without rebuilding. Enable with EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE=1.
15	"""
16	
17	import enum
18	import logging
19	import os
20	from dataclasses import dataclass
21	
22	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
23	
24	
25	class SpecMode(enum.Enum):
26	    NO_SPEC = "no_spec"
27	    MARS_D5 = "mars_d5"
28	    MARS_D7 = "mars_d7"
29	
30	
31	@dataclass(frozen=True)
32	class SpecModeConfig:
33	    enabled: bool
34	    enter_no_spec_bs: int
35	    leave_no_spec_bs: int
36	    enter_d7_bs: int
37	    leave_d7_bs: int
38	    d5_topk: int
39	    d5_steps: int
40	    d5_dtn: int
41	    d7_topk: int
42	    d7_steps: int
43	    d7_dtn: int
44	    mars_theta: float          # global fallback (legacy EAGLE_MARS_THETA)
45	    d5_mars_theta: float       # per-mode override; -1 means "use mars_theta"
46	    d7_mars_theta: float
47	
48	    def effective_d5_theta(self) -> float:
49	        return self.d5_mars_theta if self.d5_mars_theta >= 0.0 else self.mars_theta
50	
51	    def effective_d7_theta(self) -> float:
52	        return self.d7_mars_theta if self.d7_mars_theta >= 0.0 else self.mars_theta
53	
54	    def summary(self) -> str:
55	        if not self.enabled:
56	            return "EAGLE dynamic mode: DISABLED (static D5)"
57	        return (
58	            "EAGLE dynamic mode: ENABLED | "
59	            f"NO_SPEC bs>={self.enter_no_spec_bs} (leave<={self.leave_no_spec_bs}) | "
60	            f"D7 bs<={self.enter_d7_bs} (leave>={self.leave_d7_bs}) | "
61	            f"D5 (topk={self.d5_topk}, steps={self.d5_steps}, dtn={self.d5_dtn}, "
62	            f"theta={self.effective_d5_theta()}) | "
63	            f"D7 (topk={self.d7_topk}, steps={self.d7_steps}, dtn={self.d7_dtn}, "
64	            f"theta={self.effective_d7_theta()})"
65	        )
66	
67	
68	def _env_int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
69	    raw = os.environ.get(name)
70	    if raw is None or raw == "":
71	        return default
72	    try:
73	        return int(raw)
74	    except ValueError:
75	        logger.warning("[spec-mode] %s=%r not int, fallback %d", name, raw, default)
76	        return default
77	
78	
79	def _env_float(name: str, default: float) -> float:
80	    raw = os.environ.get(name)
81	    if raw is None or raw == "":
82	        return default
83	    try:
84	        return float(raw)
85	    except ValueError:
86	        logger.warning("[spec-mode] %s=%r not float, fallback %f", name, raw, default)
87	        return default
88	
89	
90	def load_config() -> SpecModeConfig:
91	    cfg = SpecModeConfig(
92	        enabled=os.environ.get("EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE", "0") == "1",
93	        enter_no_spec_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS", 31),
94	        leave_no_spec_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS", 28),
95	        enter_d7_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_BS", 1),
96	        leave_d7_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS", 3),
97	        d5_topk=_env_int("EAGLE_D5_TOPK", 2),
98	        d5_steps=_env_int("EAGLE_D5_STEPS", 2),
99	        d5_dtn=_env_int("EAGLE_D5_DTN", 5),
100	        d7_topk=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_TOPK", 2),
101	        d7_steps=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_STEPS", 3),
102	        d7_dtn=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_DTN", 7),
103	        mars_theta=_env_float("EAGLE_MARS_THETA", -1.0),
104	        # Per-mode MARS theta overrides. -1 = inherit global mars_theta.
105	        # S1 (bs=1) runs entirely in D7, so a more aggressive D7 theta can
106	        # boost S1 accept-length without affecting D5/no-spec quality.
107	        d5_mars_theta=_env_float("EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA", -1.0),
108	        d7_mars_theta=_env_float("EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA", -1.0),
109	    )
110	    _validate(cfg)
111	    return cfg
112	
113	
114	def _validate(cfg: SpecModeConfig) -> None:
115	    if not cfg.enabled:
116	        return
117	    if cfg.leave_no_spec_bs >= cfg.enter_no_spec_bs:
118	        logger.warning(
119	            "[spec-mode] leave_no_spec_bs(%d) >= enter_no_spec_bs(%d) disables hysteresis",
120	            cfg.leave_no_spec_bs, cfg.enter_no_spec_bs,
121	        )
122	    if cfg.leave_d7_bs <= cfg.enter_d7_bs:
123	        logger.warning(
124	            "[spec-mode] leave_d7_bs(%d) <= enter_d7_bs(%d) disables hysteresis",
125	            cfg.leave_d7_bs, cfg.enter_d7_bs,
126	        )
127	    for tag, topk, steps, dtn in (
128	        ("D5", cfg.d5_topk, cfg.d5_steps, cfg.d5_dtn),
129	        ("D7", cfg.d7_topk, cfg.d7_steps, cfg.d7_dtn),
130	    ):
131	        expected = 1 + topk * steps
132	        if dtn != expected:
133	            logger.warning(
134	                "[spec-mode] %s dtn=%d != 1+topk*steps=%d (chain verify expects equality)",
135	                tag, dtn, expected,
136	            )
137	
138	
139	class SpecModeSelector:
140	    """Pick spec mode for a decode batch based on running BS.
141	
142	    Hysteresis guards against prefill bursts re-entering NO_SPEC mid-bench;
143	    under monotonically-decreasing bs traffic, the path is effectively
144	    NO_SPEC -> MARS_D5 -> MARS_D7 with no backtracking.
145	    """
146	
147	    def __init__(self, config: SpecModeConfig):
148	        self.config = config
149	        self.current = SpecMode.MARS_D5  # initial default; first decode step overrides
150	
151	    def select(self, bs: int) -> SpecMode:
152	        cfg = self.config
153	        if not cfg.enabled:
154	            return SpecMode.MARS_D5
155	
156	        prev = self.current
157	        if prev == SpecMode.NO_SPEC:
158	            if bs <= cfg.leave_no_spec_bs:
159	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D7 if bs <= cfg.enter_d7_bs else SpecMode.MARS_D5
160	            else:
161	                new = SpecMode.NO_SPEC
162	        elif prev == SpecMode.MARS_D7:
163	            if bs >= cfg.enter_no_spec_bs:
164	                new = SpecMode.NO_SPEC
165	            elif bs >= cfg.leave_d7_bs:
166	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D5
167	            else:
168	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D7
169	        else:  # MARS_D5
170	            if bs >= cfg.enter_no_spec_bs:
171	                new = SpecMode.NO_SPEC
172	            elif bs <= cfg.enter_d7_bs:
173	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D7
174	            else:
175	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D5
176	
177	        if new != prev:
178	            logger.info("[spec-mode] %s -> %s (bs=%d)", prev.value, new.value, bs)
179	        self.current = new
180	        return new
181	
182	
183	def selector_from_env() -> SpecModeSelector:
184	    cfg = load_config()
185	    logger.info("[spec-mode] %s", cfg.summary())
186	    return SpecModeSelector(cfg)
187

2110	            seq_lens_cpu_cache=seq_lens_cpu

2112	
2113	        # Sliding-window draft prefill for long context (MiniCPM-SALA).
2114	        #
2115	        # When `--speculative-draft-prefix-last-n N` is set (or legacy env
2116	        # SGLANG_EAGLE_DRAFT_PREFILL_LAST_N), skip the draft forward for any
2117	        # chunked-prefill chunk that ends before the trailing N-token window
2118	        # (i.e. seq_lens_cpu[i] < req_total_lens[i] - N for all reqs in the
2119	        # batch). Only the trailing window enters draft prefill.
2120	        #
2121	        # Why this is correctness-safe (audited 2026-05-18, see also
2122	        # prefill/experiment-log.md):
2123	        #   1. EAGLE-3 chunked prefill is per-chunk independent. Each
2124	        #      `forward_draft_extend` call rebuilds `batch.spec_info` from
2125	        #      scratch using THIS chunk's target hidden state + verified_id
2126	        #      (line 2100 above). It does not read the previous chunk's
2127	        #      `spec_info.hidden_states` or `topk_*`. So skipping early
2128	        #      chunks does not corrupt later chunks' draft input.
2129	        #   2. The trailing chunk always satisfies seq_lens == req_total, so
2130	        #      the skip condition `seq_lens < req_total - N` is false for
2131	        #      any N >= 0. The trailing chunk therefore always runs draft
2132	        #      prefill normally and writes real values into spec_info before
2133	        #      decode begins.
2134	        #   3. EAGLE-3 draft decode only reads spec_info via `draft_forward`
2135	        #      step 0, which is AFTER the trailing prefill chunk overwrote
2136	        #      it. Skipped chunks never leave residual state on the read
2137	        #      path.
2138	        #   4. Draft KV slots for skipped positions are NEVER written this
2139	        #      forward. The KV pool allocator (memory_pool/allocator.py)
2140	        #      recycles freed slots without zeroing, so those positions may
2141	        #      hold residual KV from prior requests that previously occupied
2142	        #      the same slot index — i.e. garbage, not zero. Decode-time
2143	        #      draft attention does include them via paged FA, but EAGLE-3
2144	        #      draft is 1-layer self-attn dominated by local recency, so the
2145	        #      trailing N-token window dominates softmax and the residual
2146	        #      contribution is empirically negligible. Bonus: long context
2147	        #      is OOD for a draft trained on ≤2K samples; truncating to ~32K
2148	        #      moves draft input back toward the training distribution,
2149	        #      raising accept_len rather than dropping it.
2150	        #
2151	        # Wall: 524K context draft prefill 17s → ~1s (94% cut).
2152	        _last_n = self.server_args.speculative_draft_prefix_last_n
2153	        if _last_n <= 0:
2154	            _legacy_env = os.environ.get("SGLANG_EAGLE_DRAFT_PREFILL_LAST_N", "")
2155	            if _legacy_env:
2156	                try:
2157	                    _last_n = int(_legacy_env)
2158	                except (TypeError, ValueError):
2159	                    _last_n = 0
2160	        if _last_n > 0 and not batch.forward_mode.is_idle():
2161	            try:
2162	                req_total_lens = [len(r.origin_input_ids) for r in batch.reqs]
2163	            except Exception:
2164	                req_total_lens = None
2165	            seq_lens_cpu_list = (
2166	                batch.seq_lens.detach().cpu().tolist()
2167	                if batch.seq_lens is not None

> AGENT

代码读完，我把每个可搜参数的真实语义写清楚。

## 精确的参数语义（从源码逐行核对）

### 1. Ngram routing（`eagle_worker.py:1207-1261`）

实现：把 `origin_input_ids + output_ids` 整段编码成 Unicode 字符串（每 token = `chr(id+0x10000)`），用 Python `str.rfind` 做**最长后缀回溯匹配**。

- **`MIN_MATCH=5`**：触发命中的最短后缀长度。`total_len < MIN_MATCH` 直接 return None。
- **`MAX_MATCH=12`**：参与查找的最长后缀，把末尾后缀截到 `min(MAX_MATCH, total_len)`，然后从 n=MAX_MATCH 递减到 MIN_MATCH，找到第一个 rfind 命中就用。**它不是"匹配越多越好"，而是"先长后短"的搜索上界**。
- **`MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS=15`**：命中后采纳多少 token 当 draft。但 `_draft_ngram_route:1465` 把它二次 cap 成 `min(MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS, dtn-1)`。**当前 D5 dtn=7 → 实际只能用 6；D7 dtn=11 → 实际 10**。所以 15 这个数字大部分时间被 dtn 压死，没意义。
- **回退**：命中走 chain verify 跳过 draft forward；未命中走原 EAGLE draft；hit/miss 同 batch 合并到一个 `EagleVerifyInput`。target 永远 verify → **数学无损**。

### 2. Draft sliding window（`llama_eagle3.py:74-77`，`eagle_worker.py:2113-2189`）

两个独立但联动的参数：

- **`DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N=32768`**：长 context **prefill** 时只有 `seq_lens >= req_total - N` 的 chunk 跑 draft forward，前面 chunk 全 skip。末 chunk 永不 skip（注释审计在 2121-2149）。
  - skip 的 chunk 在 draft KV 池里留的是 **prior request 残留 KV**（不是 0），但 EAGLE-3 draft 是 1 层 self-attn，末 N 窗口主导 softmax，所以经验上 OK；同时长 context 是 ≤2K 训练数据的 OOD，截掉反而把分布拉回训练分布。
  - 524K → 17s 变 ~1s
- **`DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW=40960`**：runtime patch `self.self_attn.attn.sliding_window_size`，让 draft **decode** 的 FlashInfer 真截断 kv_indices 到末 sw 个 token。
  - **硬约束**：sw ≥ `prefix_last_n + chunk_size(8192)`，确保 window 起点严格在末 chunk 已写入的 KV 范围内。否则 decode 会读到没写过的 KV slot。

### 3. Spec 形状 D5/D7（`spec_mode.py:97-102`）

- D5: `topk=2 steps=3 dtn=7`；D7: `topk=2 steps=5 dtn=11`
- **硬约束**：`dtn = 1 + topk*steps`（`_validate` 会 warning）
- 改 steps/topk 时 dtn 必须跟着改
- **数学无损**（chain verify rejection sampling），但 dtn 大 → verify 一次更深、accept 期望更长，verify cost 也增加 → 是 trade-off 不是稳赚

## 修正后的可搜旋钮表

| 参数 | 当前 | 实际作用 | 备注 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NGRAM_MIN_MATCH` | 5 | 触发命中阈值 | 小=命中率高但匹配信息少（仍 target verify） |
| `NGRAM_MAX_MATCH` | 12 | 后缀搜索上界 | 大=能用更长匹配，rfind 开销略增 |
| `NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS` | 15 | **被 dtn-1 二次 cap** | 当前 D5 cap=6/D7 cap=10。除非加大 dtn 否则不要动 |
| `DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N` | 32768 | prefill 末 N 窗口 | 短 prompt 用不到；长 prompt 上是接受率 vs prefill 耗时 |
| `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW` | 40960 | decode 末 sw 真截断 | **必须 ≥ prefix_last_n + 8192** |
| `D5_STEPS/TOPK/DTN` | 3/2/7 | 中 bs 的 chain 形状 | dtn 必须 = 1+topk*steps |
| `D7_STEPS/TOPK/DTN` | 5/2/11 | bs=1 的 chain 形状 | 同上 |

## 关键耦合关系（搜索时必须注意的）

1. **`NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS` 与 `D5/D7_DTN` 耦合**：要让 ngram 用到长 chain，必须先放大 dtn（也就是加大 steps），单独调 max_draft_tokens 无效
2. **`DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N` 与 `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW` 耦合**：动 prefix_last_n 时 sliding_window 必须等量上调
3. **D5 dtn 与 NO_SPEC_BS 隐式耦合**：dtn 大 → 单步 verify token 多 → 在中 bs 区段更可能挤爆 GPU，但你说阈值不动就不展开
4. **`NGRAM_MIN_MATCH` 与 `MAX_MATCH`**：MIN ≤ MAX

729	        Args:

801	    def _set_mamba_spec_buffer(self, mode: str) -> None:
802	        """Tell the target worker's mamba pool which intermediate buffer
803	        (``"d5"`` or ``"d7"``) is active. Pure no-op for non-mamba targets
804	        or pools without dynamic-spec split. Safe to call repeatedly.
805	        """

807	        mamba_pool = getattr(pool, "mamba_pool", None) if pool is not None else None

812	            return
813	        setter(mode)

815	    def _apply_spec_config(self, mode: SpecMode) -> None:
816	        """Switch worker spec config (topk / steps / dtn) and active draft graph.
817	
818	        No-op if dynamic mode disabled or mode is the same as last applied. The
819	        draft attn_backend is shared across modes (sized to ``_max_spec_steps``
820	        at init); only the cuda-graph runner buffers differ.
821	        """
822	        cfg = self.spec_mode_config
823	        if not cfg.enabled:
824	            return
825	        if mode == SpecMode.MARS_D5:
826	            new_topk, new_steps, new_dtn = cfg.d5_topk, cfg.d5_steps, cfg.d5_dtn
827	            new_runner = self.cuda_graph_runner_d5
828	            new_backend = self.draft_attn_backend_d5
829	            new_theta = cfg.effective_d5_theta()
830	        elif mode == SpecMode.MARS_D7:
831	            new_topk, new_steps, new_dtn = cfg.d7_topk, cfg.d7_steps, cfg.d7_dtn
832	            new_runner = self.cuda_graph_runner_d7
833	            new_backend = self.draft_attn_backend_d7
834	            new_theta = cfg.effective_d7_theta()
835	        else:
836	            return  # NO_SPEC handled separately
837	
838	        # Steady-state fast path: D5 -> D5 / D7 -> D7 are the vast majority of
839	        # decode steps once the bs settles. _applied_spec_mode is None on the
840	        # very first call (forces full re-bind so cuda_graph_runner is correct
841	        # even if self.* defaults already match the requested mode).
842	        if self._applied_spec_mode == mode:
843	            return
844	
845	        self.topk = new_topk
846	        self.speculative_num_steps = new_steps
847	        self.speculative_num_draft_tokens = new_dtn
848	        self.server_args.speculative_eagle_topk = new_topk
849	        self.server_args.speculative_num_steps = new_steps
850	        self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens = new_dtn
851	        self.cuda_graph_runner = new_runner
852	        self.draft_attn_backend = new_backend
853	        self.draft_model_runner.draft_attn_backend = new_backend
854	
855	        # Switch the target-worker's mamba pool intermediate buffer so the
856	        # GLA target-verify path (eager AND cuda-graph replay) reads/writes
857	        # the correctly-sized D5 or D7 cache. No-op when the pool wasn't
858	        # initialized in dynamic-spec split mode (legacy single buffer).
859	        self._set_mamba_spec_buffer("d5" if mode == SpecMode.MARS_D5 else "d7")
860	
861	        # Per-mode MARS theta. eagle_info reads from EagleVerifyInput.mars_theta
862	        # (plumbed via draft() at construction time) so we just stash the value
863	        # on self here. D7 (typically bs=1, S1) can run a more aggressive theta
864	        # than D5 without affecting D5 quality. -1 disables MARS for that mode.
865	        self._active_mars_theta = float(new_theta)
866	        self._applied_spec_mode = mode

868	    def _forward_no_spec(self, batch: ScheduleBatch) -> GenerationBatchResult:
869	        """Run target_worker direct decode and update draft KV immediately."""
870	        bs = len(batch.reqs)
871	        if batch.output_ids is None or batch.output_ids.numel() != bs:
872	            raw_output_ids_len = (
873	                None if batch.output_ids is None else batch.output_ids.numel()
874	            )
875	            logger.warning(
876	                "[eagle-no-spec] rebuilding decode input from req tails "
877	                "(bs=%d, output_ids_len=%s, mode=%s)",
878	                bs,
879	                raw_output_ids_len,
880	                getattr(batch, "_spec_mode", None),
881	            )
882	            batch.output_ids = torch.tensor(
883	                [
884	                    (
885	                        req.output_ids[-1]
886	                        if len(req.output_ids)
887	                        else req.origin_input_ids[-1]
888	                    )
889	                    for req in batch.reqs
890	                ],
891	                dtype=torch.int64,
892	                device=self.device,
893	            )
894	
895	        # Scheduler's prepare_for_decode() early-returned because spec is
896	        # enabled — that meant EAGLE.draft() was expected to allocate the KV
897	        # slots. We're skipping draft, so run the full non-spec body now to
898	        # allocate (bs,) dense KV slots, advance seq_lens, set input_ids.
899	        # Clear any stale spec_info.positions BEFORE building model_worker_batch:
900	        # the previous step's prepare_extend_after_decode left positions sized
901	        # to the prior bs. ForwardBatch.init_new copies spec_info.positions
902	        # verbatim when set, so a stale length crashes target's input_buffers
903	        # at copy time.
904	        if isinstance(batch.spec_info, EagleDraftInput):
905	            batch.spec_info.positions = None
906	        batch.prepare_for_decode(force_no_spec=True)
907	
908	        # Target direct decode, capture FULL hidden so we can feed draft when
909	        # we eventually flush. FULL collapses to LAST under DECODE (1 token
910	        # per req); we use FULL to match target cuda_graph_runner's captured
911	        # capture_hidden_mode.
912	        batch.return_hidden_states = True
913	        model_worker_batch = batch.get_model_worker_batch()
914	        model_worker_batch.capture_hidden_mode = CaptureHiddenMode.FULL
915	        with _profile_region("EW_no_spec_target_decode"):
916	            target_result = self.target_worker.forward_batch_generation(
917	                model_worker_batch
918	            )
919	        last_hidden = target_result.logits_output.hidden_states  # (bs, hidden_aux)

1640	            capture_hidden_mode=CaptureHiddenMode.FULL,

1646	    def draft_forward(self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch):
1647	        # Parse args
1648	        spec_info = forward_batch.spec_info
1649	        assert isinstance(spec_info, EagleDraftInput)
1650	        out_cache_loc = forward_batch.out_cache_loc
1651	        topk_p, topk_index, hidden_states = (
1652	            spec_info.topk_p,
1653	            spec_info.topk_index,
1654	            spec_info.hidden_states,
1655	        )
1656	        if self.hot_token_id is not None:
1657	            topk_index = self.hot_token_id[topk_index]
1658	        # TODO: We only need self.speculative_num_steps - 1 cache loc
1659	        out_cache_loc = out_cache_loc.reshape(
1660	            forward_batch.batch_size, self.topk, self.speculative_num_steps
1661	        )
1662	        out_cache_loc = out_cache_loc.permute((2, 0, 1)).reshape(
1663	            self.speculative_num_steps, -1
1664	        )
1665	
1666	        # Return values
1667	        score_list: List[torch.Tensor] = []
1668	        token_list: List[torch.Tensor] = []
1669	        parents_list: List[torch.Tensor] = []
1670	
1671	        # Forward multiple steps
1672	        scores = None
1673	        for i in range(self.speculative_num_steps):
1674	            input_ids, hidden_states, scores, tree_info = select_top_k_tokens(
1675	                i, topk_p, topk_index, hidden_states, scores, self.topk
1676	            )
1677	            score_list.append(tree_info[0])
1678	            token_list.append(tree_info[1])
1679	            parents_list.append(tree_info[2])
1680	
1681	            # We don't need to run the last forward. we get 1 token from draft prefill and (#spec steps - 1) tokens here
1682	            if i == self.speculative_num_steps - 1:
1683	                break
1684	
1685	            # Set inputs
1686	            forward_batch.input_ids = input_ids
1687	            # This is a temporary fix for the case that the user is using standalone
1688	            # speculative decoding and the draft model architecture is gpt-oss. gpt-oss
1689	            # rope kernel needs cache_loc to be contiguous.
1690	            if (
1691	                self.server_args.speculative_algorithm == "STANDALONE"
1692	                and self.model_config.hf_config.architectures[0] == "GptOssForCausalLM"
1693	            ):
1694	                out_cache_loc = out_cache_loc.contiguous()
1695	            forward_batch.out_cache_loc = out_cache_loc[i]
1696	            forward_batch.positions.add_(1)
1697	            forward_batch.attn_backend = self.draft_attn_backend.attn_backends[i]
1698	            spec_info.hidden_states = hidden_states
1699	
1700	            # Run forward
1701	            logits_output = self.draft_model_runner.forward(
1702	                forward_batch, skip_attn_backend_init=True
1703	            ).logits_output
1704	            if self.server_args.enable_nan_detection:
1705	                detect_nan(logits_output)
1706	            probs = torch.softmax(logits_output.next_token_logits, dim=-1)
1707	            topk_p, topk_index = fast_topk(probs, self.topk, dim=-1)
1708	            if self.hot_token_id is not None:
1709	                topk_index = self.hot_token_id[topk_index]
1710	            hidden_states = logits_output.hidden_states
1711	
1712	        parent_list, top_scores_index, draft_tokens = organize_draft_results(
1713	            score_list, token_list, parents_list, self.speculative_num_draft_tokens
1714	        )
1715	
1716	        return parent_list, top_scores_index, draft_tokens

362	            # Enabled when EAGLE_MARS_THETA >= 0; default disabled (-1.0) keeps standard SD.

> AGENT

代码读透了。现在我把关键发现讲清楚，给出搜索方案，你拍板。

## 深读后修正的关键认知

### 1. 为什么 MARS theta 是精度红线（`eagle_info.py:360-397`）

verify 路径里 MARS 的真实逻辑是：

```python
accept if draft_token == top1(target_logits)              # 标准 greedy
   or  (draft_token == top2(target_logits) and z2/z1 > θ)  # MARS 扩展
```

- θ=0.85 → "z2 跟 z1 差不超过 15% 就接受 top-2"
- θ=0.5 → "z2 有 z1 一半大就接受 top-2" —— 很激进
- θ=-1 → 关 MARS，纯 greedy（数学无损）

**所以 MARS theta 直接改变输出文本**，不是"调速度"参数。用户红线正确。

### 2. 为什么 batch 切换阈值也不能动

`_apply_spec_config:861-866` 在模式切换时**同时切换 active MARS theta**：D5→0.85，D7→0.5。NO_SPEC 不跑 MARS（target 直接 decode）。所以改 NO_SPEC_BS / D7_BS 意味着改变"哪个 batch 区间用哪个 θ"，间接影响输出。用户红线也正确。

### 3. CUDA Graph 在哪里固化 dtn（关键工程约束）

`init_cuda_graphs:596-680`：D5 和 D7 **各自 capture 一份 graph**，graph 内部把 dtn 硬编码进 output shape `(bs, dtn-1)`。注释明确说：

> "the captured graph hardcodes the output shape (bs, dtn-1), so dtn MUST also match D5 here"

意味着：**改 D5_STEPS / D5_TOPK / D5_DTN 必须重启 server 让 cuda graph 重新 capture**。D7 同理。

### 4. 哪些参数真的能搜（chain verify 无损）

| 参数 | 影响哪个 bs 段 | 数学无损? | 改完要不要重启 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NGRAM_MIN_MATCH` | 全段（rfind 命中率） | ✅（target verify 兜底） | 要（`__init__` 读一次） |
| `NGRAM_MAX_MATCH` | 全段 | ✅ | 要 |
| `DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N` | 长 prompt 主导（S1/Smax 长尾） | ✅（注释审计过） | 要（server_args） |
| `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW` | 长 prompt decode | ✅ | 要（runtime patch in `__init__`） |
| `D5_STEPS/TOPK`（dtn 联动） | S8 中段 | ✅ | 要（cuda graph 重 capture） |
| `D7_STEPS/TOPK`（dtn 联动） | S1（bs=1） | ✅ | 要 |

**`NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS=15` 当前被 dtn-1 cap 死（D5=6, D7=10），单独动没意义。** 只有先把 D5_DTN / D7_DTN 调大，它才有空间。

### 5. 约束（搜索时必须自动检查）

- `dtn == 1 + topk*steps`（spec_mode `_validate` 只 warning，但 cuda graph 会按 dtn 走）
- `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW ≥ DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N + 8192`（chunk_size，硬正确性）
- `NGRAM_MIN_MATCH ≤ NGRAM_MAX_MATCH`

## 搜索方案

**单轮约 10 min（重启 ~1 min + bench ~9 min）。12h ≈ 60–65 轮。**

### Phase A — Baseline 1 轮（验证当前默认能复现）

跑 1 轮当前默认配置作锚点，避免后面对比时基线漂移。

### Phase B — 1D 坐标扫，共 ~22 轮（约 3.7h）

每条扫描线包含**当前点**和邻居，每点 1 轮：

| 维度 | 扫描点 | 轮数 |
|---|---|---|
| `NGRAM_MIN_MATCH` | {3, 4, **5**, 6, 7} | 4（去掉重复 baseline） |
| `NGRAM_MAX_MATCH` | {8, 10, **12**, 16, 20} | 4 |
| 长 ctx 联动 `(PREFIX, WINDOW)` | (16K, 24K) / **(32K, 40K)** / (48K, 56K) / (64K, 72K) / (96K, 104K) | 4 |
| D5 shape `(steps, topk, dtn)` | (2,2,5) / **(3,2,7)** / (4,2,9) / (3,3,10) / (5,2,11) | 4 |
| D7 shape | (3,2,7) / (4,2,9) / **(5,2,11)** / (6,2,13) / (5,3,16) | 4 |
| ngram 关 vs 开 | OFF 对照 | 1（验证 ngram 收益） |
| 关 ngram + draft window 默认 | 隔离 ngram 贡献 | 1 |

### Phase C — 组合验证 ~5 轮（约 50 min）

用 Phase B 各维度 top-1 组合，跑 2 轮取 median；再用 top-2 组合跑 2 轮验证不是过拟合到单维度。

### Phase D — 全量 toolkit 复验 ~3 轮（约 75 min，full bench_serving 而非 mini）

最终 top-3 配置上 `bench/data_full/` 完整跑，避免 mini 抽样误差。

**总计 ~32 轮 / 7h，留出 5h buffer 处理失败重跑、噪声评估、意外发现。**

## 待你确认

1. **方案 OK 吗？** 还是要先聚焦某个维度（例如只搜 ngram + D5/D7 shape，跳过 draft window）？
2. **每点跑 1 轮 vs 2 轮取 median**：单轮风险是 bench duration 噪声，~10min 单轮我估计噪声 1-3%；如果你要稳，每点 2 轮（双倍开销），还是先 1 轮筛掉差的 + 候选点 2 轮复测？
3. **修改 `mini_bench.sh` 默认指向 `bench/data/`** 还是用环境变量临时切？我倾向加一个 `MINI_BENCH_DATA_DIR` env，默认仍指 `data_full`，搜索脚本里 export 切到 `data`，不动现有默认。
4. **结果记录格式**：每轮我会落一个 JSON（参数 + S1/S8/Smax duration + accept_length），最终汇总成 markdown 表。需要的话也可以记 `Total tokens` 和 spec accept 比例。

数组：`{name, env_overrides, dims}`）\n  - 单轮 = 启服 → 等就绪 → 1 条 chat sanity → bench → 解析 → 记录 → 杀服\n  - 启服：`env <overrides> bash eval/start_eagle.sh > <log> 2>&1 &`\n  - 就绪判断：tail server.log grep 一次 `Uvicorn running on`（失败 pattern 同时 grep `Traceback|ERROR|Killed|OOM`），超时 12 min\n  - bench：直接调 `python3 -m sglang.bench_serving` 三档（S1 conc=1 / S8 conc=8 / Smax 无 conc 上限），数据集从 `bench/data/{c1,c8,cunlimited}.jsonl` 转换后传入\n  - 解析：抓 `Benchmark duration (s):` × 3 + 从 server.log 抓最后一次 `avg_spec_accept_length=` 的值\n  - 杀服：`bash bench/kill_sglang.sh` —— 严禁 `pkill -f sglang`（CLAUDE.md 硬规则）\n  - 失败处理：record `status=failed`，继续下一个\n- `outputs/sweep_eagle/<timestamp>/results.jsonl` — 每轮一行 JSON\n- `outputs/sweep_eagle/<timestamp>/summary.md` — 末尾汇总 markdown 表\n- `outputs/sweep_eagle/<timestamp>/server-<idx>-<name>.log` — 每轮 server 日志\n\n**不修改任何生产代码**：start_eagle.sh、demo-sala/、sglang/python/ 全部只读消费。所有可调参数都已经在 start_eagle.sh 走 env 覆盖通道。\n\n`mini_bench.sh` 不动（它默认指 `bench/data_full/` 且不接受目录 env）；driver 不调它，直接复用 `toolkit/bench_serving.sh` 的核心逻辑 inlined，或直接调 `sglang.bench_serving`。\n\n## 执行流程\n\n### Phase A — 锚点 baseline（1 轮）\n\n跑当前 start_eagle.sh 默认（不 export 任何 override），记录 S1/S8/Smax 作后续 baseline。\n\n### Phase B — 1D 坐标扫（21 轮）\n\n每个维度跑邻居点（去掉与 baseline 重复的 = 4 个新点），单点 1 轮：\n\n| 维度 | 新点数 |\n|---|---|\n| NGRAM_MIN_MATCH | 4 |\n| NGRAM_MAX_MATCH | 4 |\n| (PREFIX, SLIDING) 联动 | 4 |\n| D5 shape | 4 |\n| D7 shape | 4 |\n| ngram OFF 对照 | 1 |\n\n总 21 轮（~3.5h）。\n\n### Phase C — top 组合（5 轮）\n\ndriver 计算每维度按\"三档 duration 加权（S1:S8:Smax = 1:1:1）\"的最优点，组合成 2–3 个候选配置，每个 1 轮。再加 2 轮选当前 baseline 的复测（估噪声基线）。\n\n### Phase D — top-3 full bench（3 轮）\n\n最终 top-3 候选用 `bench/data_full/` 的完整三档（S1=12/S8=36/Smax=96）跑，确认 mini 结论不是抽样误差。\n\n**总计 ~30 轮 ≈ 5h，留 7h buffer。**\n\n## 单轮规程（driver 实现）\n\n```\n1. bash bench/kill_sglang.sh                     # 干净状态\n2. 验证无残留：pgrep -f sglang.launch_server 应空\n3. env <overrides> nohup bash eval/start_eagle.sh > <log> 2>&1 &\n4. 边 tail 边 grep \"Uvicorn running on|Traceback|ERROR|Killed|OOM\"\n   - timeout 12 min；遇 fail pattern 立即记录 + skip\n5. curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:30000/v1/models  (sanity)\n6. 发 1 条 chat：max_tokens=50，检查 response 是人话（非空、非全 unk）\n7. 转换 bench/data/*.jsonl → /tmp/sweep_<band>.jsonl（custom 格式）\n8. python3 -m sglang.bench_serving × 3 档，提取 Benchmark Duration\n9. tail -200 server.log，正则提取最后一个 avg_spec_accept_length= 值\n10. bash bench/kill_sglang.sh\n11. append 一行 JSON 到 results.jsonl\n```\n\n## 结果记录\n\n每轮 `results.jsonl` 一行：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"idx\": 7,\n  \"phase\": \"B\",\n  \"name\": \"ngram_min3\",\n  \"env_overrides\": {\"SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MIN_MATCH\": \"3\"},\n  \"S1_dur_s\": 12.34, \"S8_dur_s\": 45.67, \"Smax_dur_s\": 89.10,\n  \"avg_spec_accept_length\": 3.42,\n  \"sanity_chat_ok\": true,\n  \"status\": \"ok\",\n  \"elapsed_s\": 612,\n  \"ts\": \"2026-05-22T15:30:00\"\n}\n```\n\n`summary.md` 末尾：\n\n| Phase | Name | S1 (Δ%) | S8 (Δ%) | Smax (Δ%) | accept_len | Status |\n\nΔ% 相对 Phase A baseline，**负值代表更快（duration 下降）**。\n\n## 监控与中断恢复\n\n- driver 是同步顺序循环，每轮 append 写 JSON，崩了从 jsonl 末尾的 idx+1 续跑\n- 不开 background tail / monitor —— 用 `Monitor` 等 server 就绪时，pattern 用 `grep -m1` 匹配一次即退（CLAUDE.md 规定）；每轮一个 monitor，etc，互不串扰\n- driver 自身在前台跑（`run_in_background=true` 调用），不再 polling，全程等 driver 一次完成\n\n## 验证\n\n- **bench 数据集是否真的影响**：跑 Phase A 一轮看三档 duration 是否在历史可信范围（与 `outputs/` 里既往 baseline 对齐）\n- **driver 正确性**：先单跑 idx=0 baseline，确认产出文件齐全、accept_length 解析正确、kill_sglang 干净\n- **最终对比**：Phase D top-3 对比 baseline，相对 Δ% > 单点波动（estimated from Phase C 的 2 轮 baseline 复测）即视为真实优化\n- **可重现性**：driver 第二次跑同一 config，duration 应 ±2% 内\n\n## 待用户确认（在 ExitPlanMode 前）\n\n无 —— 用户已明确：\n1. mini_bench → bench/data/，S1=8/S8=24/Smax=64\n2. 单轮足够\n3. 12h budget\n4. VRAM 充足，不担心 OOM\n5. MARS theta 与 batch 切换阈值不动\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# EAGLE-3 + ngram 推测解码参数搜索计划

## Context

当前 `eval/start_eagle.sh` 跑出来的是默认生产配置，但其中数个 chain-verify-无损的旋钮（ngram 命中阈值、draft sliding window、D5/D7 chain 形状）从未做过系统扫描，参数空间可能没贴到 bench 的真实分布。

bench 数据严重偏长上下文（S1 中位 130K / S8 中位 92K / Smax 中位 50K），意味着：

- **draft prefix_last_n** 切到不同长度对 prefill 时间影响巨大（130K 全量 vs 32K 截断差 ~16×）；
- **D5/D7 chain 形状**决定 dtn 大小，与 ngram MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS（被 dtn-1 cap）耦合；
- **ngram 命中阈值**决定多少 step 走 chain verify 跳过 draft forward。

12h budget，单轮 mini_bench（用 `bench/data/`，S1=8/S8=24/Smax=64）~10min，理论 ≥60 轮，本计划用 ~30 轮，留 buffer 给意外。

目标：找到不动 MARS theta / batch 切换阈值前提下，三档 bench duration 综合最优的参数组合，并产出可读的对比表。

## 红线（绝对不动）

以下任一改动会改变输出文本，**整个 sweep 永远不动**：

- `EAGLE_MARS_THETA` / `EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA` / `EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA`
- `EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS` / `EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS` / `EAGLE_D7_BS` / `EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS`
- `EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE=1`
- 任何 server 启动栈、量化、kernel 派发相关 env（含 `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=48`、`SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X=0`）

依据：`eagle_info.py:360-397` MARS 接受逻辑会接受 top-2 token（直接改输出）；`_apply_spec_config:861-866` 模式切换时同步切换 active theta，所以 batch 阈值间接改输出。

## 可搜旋钮（chain verify 数学无损，仅速度/接受率 trade-off）

| 旋钮 | 当前 | 扫描点 | 实际作用（源码定位） |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MIN_MATCH` | 5 | {3, 4, **5**, 6, 7} | `eagle_worker.py:1243` 触发命中的最短后缀长度 |
| `SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_MATCH` | 12 | {8, 10, **12**, 16, 20} | `eagle_worker.py:1246-1247` rfind 搜索上界 |
| `(EAGLE_DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N, EAGLE_DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW)` | (32768, 40960) | (16K,24K)/**(32K,40K)**/(48K,56K)/(64K,72K)/(96K,104K) | `eagle_worker.py:2152` prefill 末 N 窗口 + `llama_eagle3.py:74` decode KV 真截断；硬约束 `sw ≥ prefix+8192` |
| D5 `(steps, topk, dtn)` | (3,2,7) | (2,2,5)/**(3,2,7)**/(4,2,9)/(3,3,10)/(5,2,11) | `spec_mode.py:97-99`；`init_cuda_graphs:596+` 固化 dtn 进 graph，dtn 必须 = 1+topk·steps |
| D7 `(steps, topk, dtn)` | (5,2,11) | (3,2,7)/(4,2,9)/**(5,2,11)**/(6,2,13)/(5,3,16) | `spec_mode.py:100-102`；同上约束 |
| `SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE` | 1 | {0, **1**} | `eagle_worker.py:444` 关 ngram 作对照 |

`SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_DRAFT_TOKENS=15` 当前被 `min(15, dtn-1)` cap（D5=6 / D7=10），单独动无效，不扫；只在 D5/D7 把 dtn 拉大后才考虑联动。

## Driver 约束（自动校验，违反则 skip + 警告）

- `dtn == 1 + topk * steps`（dtn 不等会 cuda graph 形状不匹配崩溃）
- `DRAFT_SLIDING_WINDOW ≥ DRAFT_PREFIX_LAST_N + 8192`
- `NGRAM_MIN_MATCH ≤ NGRAM_MAX_MATCH`

## 文件改动

**新增**：

- `bench/sweep_eagle.py` — Python 驱动器
  - 内嵌 config 列表（dict 数组：`{name, env_overrides, dims}`）
  - 单轮 = 启服 → 等就绪 → 1 条 chat sanity → bench → 解析 → 记录 → 杀服
  - 启服：`env <overrides> bash eval/start_eagle.sh > <log> 2>&1 &`
  - 就绪判断：tail server.log grep 一次 `Uvicorn running on`（失败 pattern 同时 grep `Traceback|ERROR|Killed|OOM`），超时 12 min
  - bench：直接调 `python3 -m sglang.bench_serving` 三档（S1 conc=1 / S8 conc=8 / Smax 无 conc 上限），数据集从 `bench/data/{c1,c8,cunlimited}.jsonl` 转换后传入
  - 解析：抓 `Benchmark duration (s):` × 3 + 从 server.log 抓最后一次 `avg_spec_accept_length=` 的值
  - 杀服：`bash bench/kill_sglang.sh` —— 严禁 `pkill -f sglang`（CLAUDE.md 硬规则）
  - 失败处理：record `status=failed`，继续下一个
- `outputs/sweep_eagle/<timestamp>/results.jsonl` — 每轮一行 JSON
- `outputs/sweep_eagle/<timestamp>/summary.md` — 末尾汇总 markdown 表
- `outputs/sweep_eagle/<timestamp>/server-<idx>-<name>.log` — 每轮 server 日志

**不修改任何生产代码**：start_eagle.sh、demo-sala/、sglang/python/ 全部只读消费。所有可调参数都已经在 start_eagle.sh 走 env 覆盖通道。

`mini_bench.sh` 不动（它默认指 `bench/data_full/` 且不接受目录 env）；driver 不调它，直接复用 `toolkit/bench_serving.sh` 的核心逻辑 inlined，或直接调 `sglang.bench_serving`。

## 执行流程

### Phase A — 锚点 baseline（1 轮）

跑当前 start_eagle.sh 默认（不 export 任何 override），记录 S1/S8/Smax 作后续 baseline。

### Phase B — 1D 坐标扫（21 轮）

每个维度跑邻居点（去掉与 baseline 重复的 = 4 个新点），单点 1 轮：

| 维度 | 新点数 |
|---|---|
| NGRAM_MIN_MATCH | 4 |
| NGRAM_MAX_MATCH | 4 |
| (PREFIX, SLIDING) 联动 | 4 |
| D5 shape | 4 |
| D7 shape | 4 |
| ngram OFF 对照 | 1 |

总 21 轮（~3.5h）。

### Phase C — top 组合（5 轮）

driver 计算每维度按"三档 duration 加权（S1:S8:Smax = 1:1:1）"的最优点，组合成 2–3 个候选配置，每个 1 轮。再加 2 轮选当前 baseline 的复测（估噪声基线）。

### Phase D — top-3 full bench（3 轮）

最终 top-3 候选用 `bench/data_full/` 的完整三档（S1=12/S8=36/Smax=96）跑，确认 mini 结论不是抽样误差。

**总计 ~30 轮 ≈ 5h，留 7h buffer。**

## 单轮规程（driver 实现）

```
1. bash bench/kill_sglang.sh                     # 干净状态
2. 验证无残留：pgrep -f sglang.launch_server 应空
3. env <overrides> nohup bash eval/start_eagle.sh > <log> 2>&1 &
4. 边 tail 边 grep "Uvicorn running on|Traceback|ERROR|Killed|OOM"
   - timeout 12 min；遇 fail pattern 立即记录 + skip
5. curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:30000/v1/models  (sanity)
6. 发 1 条 chat：max_tokens=50，检查 response 是人话（非空、非全 unk）
7. 转换 bench/data/*.jsonl → /tmp/sweep_<band>.jsonl（custom 格式）
8. python3 -m sglang.bench_serving × 3 档，提取 Benchmark Duration
9. tail -200 server.log，正则提取最后一个 avg_spec_accept_length= 值
10. bash bench/kill_sglang.sh
11. append 一行 JSON 到 results.jsonl
```

## 结果记录

每轮 `results.jsonl` 一行：

```json
{
  "idx": 7,
  "phase": "B",
  "name": "ngram_min3",
  "env_overrides": {"SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MIN_MATCH": "3"},
  "S1_dur_s": 12.34, "S8_dur_s": 45.67, "Smax_dur_s": 89.10,
  "avg_spec_accept_length": 3.42,
  "sanity_chat_ok": true,
  "status": "ok",
  "elapsed_s": 612,
  "ts": "2026-05-22T15:30:00"
}
```

`summary.md` 末尾：

| Phase | Name | S1 (Δ%) | S8 (Δ%) | Smax (Δ%) | accept_len | Status |

Δ% 相对 Phase A baseline，**负值代表更快（duration 下降）**。

## 监控与中断恢复

- driver 是同步顺序循环，每轮 append 写 JSON，崩了从 jsonl 末尾的 idx+1 续跑
- 不开 background tail / monitor —— 用 `Monitor` 等 server 就绪时，pattern 用 `grep -m1` 匹配一次即退（CLAUDE.md 规定）；每轮一个 monitor，etc，互不串扰
- driver 自身在前台跑（`run_in_background=true` 调用），不再 polling，全程等 driver 一次完成

## 验证

- **bench 数据集是否真的影响**：跑 Phase A 一轮看三档 duration 是否在历史可信范围（与 `outputs/` 里既往 baseline 对齐）
- **driver 正确性**：先单跑 idx=0 baseline，确认产出文件齐全、accept_length 解析正确、kill_sglang 干净
- **最终对比**：Phase D top-3 对比 baseline，相对 Δ% > 单点波动（estimated from Phase C 的 2 轮 baseline 复测）即视为真实优化
- **可重现性**：driver 第二次跑同一 config，duration 应 ±2% 内

## 待用户确认（在 ExitPlanMode 前）

无 —— 用户已明确：

2. 单轮足够
3. 12h budget
4. VRAM 充足，不担心 OOM
5. MARS theta 与 batch 切换阈值不动

tool_result Bash
```
#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
# Kill sglang server safely — 只杀 launch_server 进程及其子进程，不碰 jupyter
# 用法: bash bench/kill_sglang.sh

# 找到 sglang.launch_server 的主进程（排除 grep 自身和 jupyter）
# `ps aux` 在某些环境里会截断命令行，导致 grep 匹配失败；改用 args 全长输出。
find_sglang_pids() {
    ps -eo pid=,args= \
        | grep '[s]glang.launch_server' \
        | grep -v jupyter \
        | awk '{print $1}' \
        || true
}

PIDS=$(find_sglang_pids)

if [ -z "$PIDS" ]; then
    echo "No sglang launch_server running"
    exit 0
fi

for PID in $PIDS; do
    echo "Killing sglang PID=$PID and its children"
    # 先杀子进程树，再杀主进程
    pkill -P "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
    kill "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
done

# 等待退出
for i in $(seq 1 15); do
    REMAINING=$(find_sglang_pids)
    if [ -z "$REMAINING" ]; then
        echo "Server stopped"
        exit 0
    fi
    sleep 1
done

# 强制杀
```

10, 16, 20]:\n        cfgs.append(Config(f\"ngram_max{v}\", \"B\",\n                           {\"SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_MAX_MATCH\": str(v)}))\n\n    # (PREFIX, SLIDING): (16K,24K) (32K,40K)* (48K,56K) cfgs.append(Config(f\"win_p{p//1024}k\", \"B\", _window_env(p, w)))\n\n    # D5 shape: (2,2,5) (3,2,7)* (4,2,9) (3,3,10) (5,2,11)\n    for topk, steps, dtn in [(2, 2, 5), (2, 4, 9), (3, 3, 10), (2, 5, 11)]:\n        assert _dtn_ok(topk, steps, dtn)\n        cfgs.append(Config(f\"d5_s{steps}k{topk}\", \"B\",\n                           _shape_env(\"D5\", topk, steps, dtn)))\n\n    # D7 shape: (3,2,7) (4,2,9) (5,2,11)* (6,2,13) (5,3,16)\n    for topk, steps, dtn in [(2, 3, 7), (2, 4, 9), (2, 6, 13), (3, 5, 16)]:\n        assert _dtn_ok(topk, steps, dtn)\n        cfgs.append(Config(f\"d7_s{steps}k{topk}\", \"B\",\n                           _shape_env(\"D7\", topk, steps, dtn)))\n\n    # ngram OFF 对照\n    cfgs.append(Config(\"ngram_off\", \"B\",\n                       {\"SGLANG_EAGLE_NGRAM_ROUTE\": \"0\"},\n                       notes=\"对照：关 ngram 走纯 EAGLE-3 draft\"))\n\n    return cfgs\n\n\n# ----------------------------- 工具 -------------------------------------------\n\ndef now_iso() -> str:\n    return datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\")\n\n\ndef log(msg: str) -> None:\n    print(f\"[{now_iso()}] {msg}\", flush=True)\n\n\ndef kill_server() -> None:\n    subprocess.run([\"bash\", str(KILL_SH)], check=False,\n                   stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)\n\n\ndef server_running() -> bool:\n    r = subprocess.run(\n        \"ps -eo pid=,args= | grep '[s]glang.launch_server' | grep -v jupyter\",\n        shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True\n    )\n    return bool(r.stdout.strip())\n\n\ndef curl_models_ok() -> bool:\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{SERVER_URL}/v1/models\", timeout=5) as r:\n            return r.status == 200\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef sanity_chat() -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"发一条 chat,验证 response 非空非全 unk.\"\"\"\n    payload = json.dumps({\n        \"model\": \"minicpm\",\n        \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"你好,请用一句话介绍你自己。\"}],\n        \"max_tokens\": 50,\n        \"temperature\": 0.0,\n    }).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(\n        f\"{SERVER_URL}/v1/chat/completions\",\n        data=payload,\n        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as r:\n            body = json.loads(r.read().decode())\n        text = body[\"choices\"][0][\"message\"][\"content\"]\n        if not text or len(text.strip()) < 4:\n            return False, f\"too short: {text!r}\"\n        # 检查不是全 unk / 乱码\n        if text.count(\"<unk>\") > 5 or text.count(\"�\") > 5:\n            return False, f\"unk/garbage: {text[:120]!r}\"\n        return True, text[:120]\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, f\"exc: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\"\n\n\ndef convert_dataset(src: Path, dst: Path) -> int:\n    n = 0\n    with open(src) as fin, open(dst, \"w\") as fout:\n        for line in fin:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            item = json.loads(line)\n            prompt = (item.get(\"question\") or item.get(\"input\")\n                      or item.get(\"prompt\"))\n            if prompt is None:\n                raise KeyError(f\"missing prompt in {src}\")\n            conv = {\n                \"conversations\": [\n                    {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt},\n                    {\"role\": \"assistant\",\n                     \"content\": item.get(\"model_response\", \"placeholder\")},\n                ]\n            }\n            fout.write(json.dumps(conv, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            n += 1\n    return n\n\n\ndef run_bench_one(band: str, conc: Optional[int], data: Path, n: int,\n                  log_path: Path) -> Optional[float]:\n    \"\"\"跑一档 bench_serving,返回 Benchmark Duration(s)。失败返回 None。\"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        \"python3\", \"-m\", \"sglang.bench_serving\",\n        \"--backend\", \"sglang\",\n        \"--host\", SERVER_HOST,\n        \"--port\", str(SERVER_PORT),\n        \"--dataset-name\", \"custom\",\n        \"--dataset-path\", str(data),\n        \"--num-prompts\", str(n),\n        \"--flush-cache\",\n    ]\n    if conc is not None:\n        cmd += [\"--max-concurrency\", str(conc)]\n    log(f\"  [{band}] cmd: {' '.join(shlex.quote(c) for c in cmd)}\")\n    with open(log_path, \"ab\") as logf:\n        logf.write(f\"\\n===== {band} =====\\n\".encode())\n        logf.flush()\n        try:\n            r = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,\n                               stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,\n                               timeout=BENCH_TIMEOUT_S, check=False)\n        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n            log(f\"  [{band}] TIMEOUT after {BENCH_TIMEOUT_S}s\")\n            return None\n        out = r.stdout.decode(errors=\"replace\")\n        logf.write(out.encode())\n    m = BENCH_DUR_RE.search(out)\n    if not m:\n        log(f\"  [{band}] no Benchmark Duration in output\")\n        return None\n    return float(m.group(1))\n\n\ndef parse_accept_length(server_log: Path) -> Optional[float]:\n    \"\"\"从 server.log 末尾找最后一个 avg_spec_accept_length=X.Y\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(server_log, \"rb\") as f:\n            f.seek(0, 2)\n            size = f.tell()\n            f.seek(max(0, size - 200_000))  # 末 200KB\n            tail = f.read().decode(errors=\"replace\")\n    except Exception:\n        return None\n    vals = ACCEPT_LEN_RE.findall(tail)\n    return float(vals[-1]) if vals else None\n\n\ndef start_server(env_overrides: Dict[str, str], server_log: Path) -> subprocess.Popen:\n    env = os.environ.copy()\n    env.update(env_overrides)\n    env[\"PYTHONUNBUFFERED\"] = \"1\"\n    fout = open(server_log, \"wb\")\n    p = subprocess.Popen(\n        [\"bash\", str(START_SH)],\n        stdout=fout, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,\n        env=env, cwd=str(REPO), start_new_session=True,\n    )\n    return p\n\n\ndef wait_ready(server_log: Path, deadline: float) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"tail server_log 直到 ready 或失败。返回 (ok, reason).\"\"\"\n    pos = 0\n    last_print = time.time()\n    while time.time() < deadline:\n        try:\n            with open(server_log, \"rb\") as f:\n                f.seek(pos)\n                chunk = f.read()\n                pos = f.tell()\n        except FileNotFoundError:\n            chunk = b\"\"\n        if chunk:\n            text = chunk.decode(errors=\"replace\")\n            for pat in READY_PATTERNS:\n                if pat.search(text):\n                    # 额外 curl 校验\n                    for _ in range(20):\n                        if curl_models_ok():\n                            return True, \"uvicorn + curl ok\"\n                        time.sleep(0.5)\n                    return False, \"uvicorn pattern matched but curl /v1/models failed\"\n            for pat in FAIL_PATTERNS:\n                if pat.search(text):\n                    return False, f\"fail pattern: {pat.pattern}\"\n        if time.time() - last_print > 30:\n            log(\"  ...still waiting for server\")\n            last_print = time.time()\n        time.sleep(1)\n    return False, f\"timeout after {READY_TIMEOUT_S}s\"\n\n\n# ----------------------------- 主循环 -----------------------------------------\n\ndef run_one(idx: int, cfg: Config, out_dir: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    t0 = time.time()\n    log(\"=\" * 60)\n    log(f\"idx={idx} phase={cfg.phase} name={cfg.name}\")\n    if cfg.env_overrides:\n        log(f\"  env: {cfg.env_overrides}\")\n    if cfg.notes:\n        log(f\"  note: {cfg.notes}\")\n\n    rec: Dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"idx\": idx,\n        \"phase\": cfg.phase,\n        \"name\": cfg.name,\n        \"env_overrides\": cfg.env_overrides,\n        \"bench_data\": cfg.bench_data,\n        \"S1_dur_s\": None, \"S8_dur_s\": None, \"Smax_dur_s\": None,\n        \"avg_spec_accept_length\": None,\n        \"sanity_chat_ok\": None,\n        \"sanity_chat_preview\": \"\",\n        \"status\": \"running\",\n        \"fail_reason\": \"\",\n        \"elapsed_s\": 0,\n        \"ts\": now_iso(),\n    }\n\n    server_log = out_dir / f\"server-{idx:02d}-{cfg.name}.log\"\n\n    # 1) kill 残留\n    kill_server()\n    time.sleep(2)\n    if server_running():\n        log(\"  !! server still running after kill, force kill\")\n        kill_server()\n        time.sleep(3)\n\n    # 2) start\n    log(f\"  start_eagle.sh -> {server_log.name}\")\n    proc = start_server(cfg.env_overrides, server_log)\n    deadline = time.time() + READY_TIMEOUT_S\n    ok, reason = wait_ready(server_log, deadline)\n    if not ok:\n        rec[\"status\"] = \"start_failed\"\n        rec[\"fail_reason\"] = reason\n        log(f\"  start FAILED: {reason}\")\n        kill_server()\n        rec[\"elapsed_s\"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)\n        return rec\n    log(f\"  ready: {reason}\")\n\n    # 3) sanity chat\n    ok, preview = sanity_chat()\n    rec[\"sanity_chat_ok\"] = ok\n    rec[\"sanity_chat_preview\"] = preview\n    if not ok:\n        log(f\"  sanity FAILED: {preview}\")\n        rec[\"status\"] = \"sanity_failed\"\n        rec[\"fail_reason\"] = preview\n        kill_server()\n        rec[\"elapsed_s\"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)\n        return rec\n    log(f\"  sanity ok: {preview}\")\n\n    # 4) bench x3\n    data_dir = DATA_DIR_MINI if cfg.bench_data == \"mini\" else DATA_DIR_FULL\n    bands = [\n        (\"S1\", 1, data_dir / \"speed_bench_c1.jsonl\"),\n        (\"S8\", 8, data_dir / \"speed_bench_c8.jsonl\"),\n        (\"Smax\", None, data_dir / \"speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl\"),\n    ]\n    bench_log = out_dir / f\"bench-{idx:02d}-{cfg.name}.log\"\n    for band, conc, src in bands:\n        tmp = Path(f\"/tmp/sweep_{band}_{idx}.jsonl\")\n        try:\n            n = convert_dataset(src, tmp)\n        except Exception as e:\n            log(f\"  [{band}] dataset convert failed: {e}\")\n            rec[f\"{band}_dur_s\"] = None\n            continue\n        dur = run_bench_one(band, conc, tmp, n, bench_log)\n        rec[f\"{band}_dur_s\"] = dur\n        log(f\"  [{band}] duration = {dur}\")\n\n    # 5) accept_length\n    rec[\"avg_spec_accept_length\"] = parse_accept_length(server_log)\n    log(f\"  accept_length = {rec['avg_spec_accept_length']}\")\n\n    # 6) kill\n    kill_server()\n    time.sleep(2)\n\n    # 判断状态\n    if any(rec[k] is None for k in (\"S1_dur_s\", \"S8_dur_s\", \"Smax_dur_s\")):\n        rec[\"status\"] = \"partial\"\n    else:\n        rec[\"status\"] = \"ok\"\n\n    rec[\"elapsed_s\"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)\n    log(f\"  done in {rec['elapsed_s']}s status={rec['status']}\")\n    return rec\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--out-dir\", required=True,\n                    help=\"输出目录,会自动创建\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--only\", default=None,\n                    help=\"只跑指定 name(可逗号分隔)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--from-idx\", type=int, default=0)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--resume\", action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"续跑：跳过 results.jsonl 已有的 idx\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"只打印 config 不执行\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir).resolve()\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    results = out_dir / \"results.jsonl\"\n\n    configs = build_configs()\n    if args.only:\n        only = set(args.only.split(\",\"))\n        configs = [c for c in configs if c.name in only]\n\n    done_idx: set[int] = set()\n    if args.resume and results.exists():\n        for line in results.open():\n            try:\n                done_idx.add(json.loads(line)[\"idx\"])\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    log(f\"total configs: {len(configs)}; out_dir: {out_dir}\")\n    if args.dry_run:\n        for i, c in enumerate(configs):\n            print(f\"  {i:02d} [{c.phase}] {c.name:25s} env={c.env_overrides}\")\n        return 0\n\n    # 安全检查\n    if not START_SH.is_file():\n        log(f\"missing {START_SH}\"); return 2\n    if not KILL_SH.is_file():\n        log(f\"missing {KILL_SH}\"); return 2\n\n    for idx, cfg in enumerate(configs):\n        if idx < args.from_idx:\n            continue\n        if idx in done_idx:\n            log(f\"skip idx={idx} (already in results.jsonl)\")\n            continue\n        try:\n            rec = run_one(idx, cfg, out_dir)\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            log(\"interrupted by user\")\n            kill_server()\n            return 130\n        except Exception as e:\n            log(f\"!! unhandled exception: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n            rec = {\n                \"idx\": idx, \"phase\": cfg.phase, \"name\": cfg.name,\n                \"env_overrides\": cfg.env_overrides,\n                \"status\": \"exception\", \"fail_reason\": f\"{type(e).__name__}: {e}\",\n                \"ts\": now_iso(),\n            }\n            kill_server()\n        with results.open(\"a\") as f:\n            f.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n        log(f\"appended idx={idx} to {results.name}\")\n\n    # 确保杀干净\n    kill_server()\n    log(\"ALL DONE\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}
